The new owner authenticates its source/helper closure, complete native producer, exact original/minmax artifacts, shared prompts and reply reservations, context and user memory controls. Adjacent alternating pairs use separate sequential native processes; profile order reverses each round. New output is mandatory and a failed launched cell cannot be replaced. Complete work and physical receipts are required before analysis. A final study additionally requires a frozen complete eligible pilot analysis, sampling rationale and enough run-level samples for its simultaneous median bound. Pilot results cannot pass the final speed gate or qualify an artifact.

Each native process has parent supervision for actual process footprint, normal OS pressure, real headroom, time, receipt storage and disk reserve. Cleanup drains an owned child before the next arm and retains startup/failure evidence. After exit, the owner waits past the host-statistics cache before sampling the next process's headroom. The process observer stores numeric CPU observations and known job kinds, never command lines. Its clean-timing threshold is fifty percent of one CPU core as a sampled recent average; it is an exclusion rule, not a safety boundary or proof that lower activity is harmless.

Execution failure ends the campaign with incomplete evidence. Timing interference retains functional results and all remaining planned cells, but prevents an aggregate claim from a favorable subset. Paging, power/thermal changes and allocation changes from the original plan are separate timing exclusions. Raw token/text evidence remains immutable. Analysis reports every scenario, paired ratios, startup separately by memory profile, single-token latency and the worst observed stall. It never treats callback batches or correlated token intervals as independent performance trials. Fresh application caches do not imply a cold operating-system file cache.

Nine fixture groups pass on both Python runtimes, including real tiny child completion, forced physical-limit cancellation with confirmed cleanup, post-exit observation failure, immutable attempted output, complete paired coverage, tampered evidence, shared-work refusal, nonfinite/counter handling through the metric helper, resource and reservation failure, and no favorable timing subset. Thirty-two static entry checks pass with registration. The initial run failed because the test's broad subprocess stub also replaced the cleanup helper's process table. The corrected fixture stubs only the intended pressure command; the failed log remains preserved. No model, compiler, payload scan or performance campaign was launched.

Full engine weights-free, instrumented coverage and external-library CI, and full Mac checks/Xcode CI, pass for 0435d4871743c1d9a215fd8e3c500c2272eb7c0d. This establishes compilation and integration of the explicit affine lookahead preparation, not its physical parity or speed benefit. The newer native timing source at 4d6ae7a8f8549c614821e14c08d6fcd67bdab715 remains in CI at this checkpoint. Physical lookahead proof, complete configuration pilot/final freeze, measured lower-budget profiles and model qualification remain open. The running held-out quality continuation remains unchanged and unanalyzed.
